return '[' + ' '.join(f'{x:>{w}.{p}f}' for x in v) + ']'\\n\\n\\ndef fm(th, mc, se, k=2):\\n    ok = np.abs(mc - th) < k * se\\n    return ''.join('✓' if o else '✗' for o in ok), ok.all()\\n\\n\\n# plim = (E[Z'Z])^{-1} E[Z'X] beta\\n#\\n# Key moments:\\n#   q_t = C @ p_{A|T=t}           (P(A*|T=t))\\n#   q_marg = 0.5*(q_0 + q_1)\\n#   E[D*_j * D_k] = C[j,k] * p_marg[k]\\n#  M | T ~ Cat(softmax(gamma + delta*T))\\n#        Y = beta1*D_{M,1} + beta2*D_{M,2} + lambda*U + eps\\n#        M* | M ~ Cat(C[:, M])\\n#\\n# Researcher regression:  Y ~ 1 + T + D*_{M,1} + D*_{M,2}\\n#\\n# T correlated with U (confounding), so E[Z'(lambda*U)] != 0.\\n# Use large-sample moment approximation:\\n#   plim = (E[Z'Z])^{-1} E[Z'Y]   estimated from N_LARGE samples.\\n#\\n# For fast random search, pre-compute sufficient statistics:\\n#   E[D_{M,k}], E[D_{M,k}*T], E[D_{M,k}*Y]  for k=0,1,2\\n# Then for any C:\\n#   E[D*_j] = sum_k C[j,k]*E[D_{M,k}]   etc.\\n# ================================================================\\ndef _gen_fd(N, beta, rng, g=GAMMA_FD, d=DELTA_FD,\\n            aU=ALPHA_U, lU=LAMBDA_U):\\n    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    T = aU * U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    lo = np.column_stack([np.zeros(N), g[1] + d[1]*T, g[2] + d[2]*T])\\n    lo -= lo.max(1, keepdims=True)\\n    e = np.exp(lo)\\n    p = e / e.sum(1, keepdims=True)\\n    cu = np.cumsum(p, axis=1)\\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)\\n    M = (u >= cu[:, 0]).astype(int) + (u >= cu[:, 1]).astype(int)\\n    DM1 = (M == 1).astype(float)\\n    DM2 = (M == 2).astype(float)\\n    Y = beta[1]*DM1 + beta[2]*DM2 + lU*U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    return T, M, Y, U\\n\\n\\ndef precomp_fd(beta, N=N_LARGE, seed=123):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n    DM = np.zeros((N, K))\\n    for k in range(K):\\n        DM[:, k] = (M == k).astype(float)\\n    return dict(\\n        E_T=T.mean(), E_T2=(T**2).mean(),\\n        E_Y=Y.mean(), E_TY=(T*Y).mean(),\\n        E_DM=DM.mean(0),\\n        E_DM_T=(DM * T[:, None]).mean(0),\\n        E_DM_Y=(DM * Y[:, None]).mean(0))\\n\\n\\ndef plim_fd(C, st):\\n    ds  = C @ st['E_DM']\\n    dst = C @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    dsy = C @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.array([\\n        [1,      ET,      ds[1],    ds[2]   ],\\n        [ET,     ET2,     dst[1],   dst[2]  ],\\n        [ds[1],  dst[1],  ds[1],    0       ],\\n        [ds[2],  dst[2],  0,        ds[2]   ]])\\n\\n    EZY = np.array([EY, ETY, dsy[1], dsy[2]])\\n    return np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY)\\n\\n\\ndef mc_fd(C, beta, nr=N_REPS, N=N_MC, seed=42):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    out = np.zeros((nr, 4))\\n    for r in range(nr):\\n        T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n        Ms = gen_Astar(M, C, rng)\\n        Z = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T,\\n                             (Ms == 1).astype(float),\\n                             (Ms == 2).astype(float)])\\n        out[r] = ols(Z, Y)\\n    return out.mean(0), out.std(0) / np.sqrt(nr)\\n\\n\\ndef rsearch_fd(st, ns=N_RANDOM, seed=999):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    clean = plim_fd(np.eye(K), st)\\n\\n    Cs = np.zeros((ns, K, K))\\n    for i in range(K):\\n        Cs[:, :, i] = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=ns)\\n\\n    E_DMs   = Cs @ st['E_DM']\\n    E_DMs_T = Cs @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    E_DMs_Y = Cs @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.zeros((ns, 4, 4))\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 0] = 1;       EZZ[:, 0, 1] = EZZ[:, 1, 0] = ET\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 0] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 0] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 1] = ET2\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 2, 2] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 3, 3] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n\\n    EZY = np.zeros((ns, 4))\\n    EZY[:, 0] = EY;   EZY[:, 1] = ETY\\n    EZY[:, 2] = E_DMs_Y[:, 1]\\n    EZY[:, 3] = E_DMs_Y[:, 2]\\n\\n    pl = np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY[:, :, None])[:, :, 0]\\n\\n    df = np.abs(pl - clean)\\n    viol = np.any(df > .01, 1).mean()\\n    nz = np.abs(clean) > 1e-6\\n    sign = np.any(np.sign(pl[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), 1).mean()\\n    amp = np.any(np.abs(pl) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, 1).mean()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.04}}"

beta1*D_{M,1} + beta2*D_{M,2} + lambda*U + eps\\n#        M* | M ~ Cat(C[:, M])\\n#\\n# Researcher regression:  Y ~ 1 + T + D*_{M,1} + D*_{M,2}\\n#\\n# T correlated with U (confounding), so E[Z'(lambda*U)] != 0.\\n# Use large-sample moment approximation:\\n#   plim = (E[Z'Z])^{-1} E[Z'Y]   estimated from N_LARGE samples.\\n#\\n# For fast random search, pre-compute sufficient statistics:\\n#   E[D_{M,k}], E[D_{M,k}*T], E[D_{M,k}*Y]  for k=0,1,2\\n# Then for any C:\\n#   E[D*_j] = sum_k C[j,k]*E[D_{M,k}]   etc.\\n# ================================================================\\ndef _gen_fd(N, beta, rng, g=GAMMA_FD, d=DELTA_FD,\\n            aU=ALPHA_U, lU=LAMBDA_U):\\n    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    T = aU * U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    lo = np.column_stack([np.zeros(N), g[1] + d[1]*T, g[2] + d[2]*T])\\n    lo -= lo.max(1, keepdims=True)\\n    e = np.exp(lo)\\n    p = e / e.sum(1, keepdims=True)\\n    cu = np.cumsum(p, axis=1)\\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)\\n    M = (u >= cu[:, 0]).astype(int) + (u >= cu[:, 1]).astype(int)\\n    DM1 = (M == 1).astype(float)\\n    DM2 = (M == 2).astype(float)\\n    Y = beta[1]*DM1 + beta[2]*DM2 + lU*U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    return T, M, Y, U\\n\\n\\ndef precomp_fd(beta, N=N_LARGE, seed=123):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n    DM = np.zeros((N, K))\\n    for k in range(K):\\n        DM[:, k] = (M == k).astype(float)\\n    return dict(\\n        E_T=T.mean(), E_T2=(T**2).mean(),\\n        E_Y=Y.mean(), E_TY=(T*Y).mean(),\\n        E_DM=DM.mean(0),\\n        E_DM_T=(DM * T[:, None]).mean(0),\\n        E_DM_Y=(DM * Y[:, None]).mean(0))\\n\\n\\ndef plim_fd(C, st):\\n    ds  = C @ st['E_DM']\\n    dst = C @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    dsy = C @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.array([\\n        [1,      ET,      ds[1],    ds[2]   ],\\n        [ET,     ET2,     dst[1],   dst[2]  ],\\n        [ds[1],  dst[1],  ds[1],    0       ],\\n        [ds[2],  dst[2],  0,        ds[2]   ]])\\n\\n    EZY = np.array([EY, ETY, dsy[1], dsy[2]])\\n    return np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY)\\n\\n\\ndef mc_fd(C, beta, nr=N_REPS, N=N_MC, seed=42):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    out = np.zeros((nr, 4))\\n    for r in range(nr):\\n        T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n        Ms = gen_Astar(M, C, rng)\\n        Z = np.column_stack([np.ones(N), T,\\n                             (Ms == 1).astype(float),\\n                             (Ms == 2).astype(float)])\\n        out[r] = ols(Z, Y)\\n    return out.mean(0), out.std(0) / np.sqrt(nr)\\n\\n\\ndef rsearch_fd(st, ns=N_RANDOM, seed=999):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    clean = plim_fd(np.eye(K), st)\\n\\n    Cs = np.zeros((ns, K, K))\\n    for i in range(K):\\n        Cs[:, :, i] = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=ns)\\n\\n    E_DMs    EZZ[:, 0, 0] = 1;       EZZ[:, 0, 1] = EZZ[:, 1, 0] = ET\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 0] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 0] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 1] = ET2\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 2, 2] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 3, 3] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n\\n    EZY = np.zeros((ns, 4))\\n    EZY[:, 0] = EY;   EZY[:, 1] = ETY\\n    EZY[:, 2] = E_DMs_Y[:, 1]\\n    EZY[:, 3] = E_DMs_Y[:, 2]\\n\\n    pl = np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY[:, :, None])[:, :, 0]\\n\\n    df = np.abs(pl - clean)\\n    viol = return viol, sign, amp\\n\\n\\n# ================================================================\\n# Main\\n# ================================================================\\ndef main():\\n    t0 = Researcher regression:  Y ~ 1 + T + D*_{M,1} + D*_{M,2}\\n#\\n# T correlated with U (confounding), so E[Z'(lambda*U)] != 0.\\n# Use large-sample moment approximation:\\n#   plim = (E[Z'Z])^{-1} E[Z'Y] C[j,k]*E[D_{M,k}]   etc.\\n# ================================================================\\ndef _gen_fd(N, beta, rng, g=GAMMA_FD, d=DELTA_FD,\\n            aU=ALPHA_U, lU=LAMBDA_U):\\n    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    T = aU * U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    lo = np.column_stack([np.zeros(N), g[1] + d[1]*T, g[2] + d[2]*T])\\n    lo -= lo.max(1, keepdims=True)\\n    e = np.exp(lo)\\n    p = e / e.sum(1, keepdims=True)\\n    cu = np.cumsum(p, axis=1)\\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)\\n    M = (u >= cu[:, 0]).astype(int) + (u >= cu[:, 1]).astype(int)\\n    DM1 = (M == 1).astype(float)\\n    DM2 = (M == 2).astype(float)\\n    Y = beta[1]*DM1 + beta[2]*DM2 + lU*U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    return T, M, Y, U\\n\\n\\ndef precomp_fd(beta, N=N_LARGE, seed=123):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n    DM = np.zeros((N, K))\\n    for k in range(K):\\n        DM[:, k] = (M == k).astype(float)\\n    return dict(\\n        E_T=T.mean(), E_T2=(T**2).mean(),\\n        E_Y=Y.mean(), E_TY=(T*Y).mean(),\\n        E_DM=DM.mean(0),\\n        E_DM_T=(DM * T[:, None]).mean(0),\\n        E_DM_Y=(DM * Y[:, None]).mean(0))\\n\\n\\ndef plim_fd(C, st):\\n    ds  = C @ st['E_DM']\\n    dst = C @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    dsy = C @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.array([\\n        [1,      ET,      ds[1],    ds[2]   ],\\n        [ET,     ET2,     dst[1],   dst[2]  ],\\n        [ds[1],  dst[1],  ds[1],    0       ],\\n        [ds[2],  dst[2],  0,        ds[2]   ]])\\n\\n    EZY = np.array([EY, ETY, dsy[1], dsy[2]])\\n    return np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY)\\n\\n\\ndef mc_fd(C, beta, nr=N_REPS, N=N_MC, s\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 428 字符] ...\\n\\n=N_RANDOM, seed=999):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    clean = plim_fd(np.eye(K), st)\\n\\n    Cs = np.zeros((ns, K, K))\\n    for i in range(K):\\n        Cs[:, :, i] = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=ns)\\n\\n    E_DMs   = Cs @ st['E_DM']\\n    E_DMs_T = Cs @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    E_DMs_Y = Cs @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.zeros((ns, 4, 4))\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 0] = 1;       EZZ[:, 0, 1] = EZZ[:, 1, 0] = ET\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 0] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 0] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 1] = ET2\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 2, 2] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 3, 3] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n\\n    EZY = np.zeros((ns, 4))\\n    EZY[:, 0] = EY;   EZY[:, 1] = ETY\\n    EZY[:, 2] = E_DMs_Y[:, 1]\\n    EZY[:, 3] = E_DMs_Y[:, 2]\\n\\n    pl = np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY[:, :, None])[:, :, 0]\\n\\n    df = np.abs(pl - clean)\\n    viol = np.any(df > .01, 1).mean()\\n    nz = np.abs(clean) > 1e-6\\n    sign = np.any(np.sign(pl[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), 1).mean()\\n    amp = np.any(np.abs(pl) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, 1).mean()\\n    return viol, sign, amp\\n\\n\\n# ================================================================\\n# Main\\n# ================================================================\\ndef main():\\n    t0 = time.time()\\n    np.set_printoptions(precision=4, suppress=True)\\n\\n    for bn, beta in [(\\\"mixed [0,1,-0.5]\\\", BETA_MIXED),\\n                      (\\\"same [0,1,0.5]\\\",   BETA_SAME)]:\\n\\n        # ---- DAG 1: Mediation ----\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*90}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  MEDIATION: T -> A -> Y  |  beta={bn}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'='*90}\\\")\\n        p0, p1 = p_cat(0, GAMMA_MED, DELTA_MED), p_cat(1, GAMMA_MED, DELTA_MED)\\n        print(f\\\"  P(A|T=0)={fv(p0,6,3)}  P(A|T=1)={fv(p1,6,3)}  tau={TAU}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Coefs: [{', '.join(COEF_NAMES)}]\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n        hdr = f\\\"  {'C':<14} {'Theory':>36}  {'MC mean':>36}  Mt\\\"\\n        print(hdr)\\n        print(f\\\"  {'-'*92}\\\")\\n\\n        all_ok = True\\n        for cn, C in C_DICT.items():\\n            th = plim_med(C, beta, TAU)\\n            mc, se = mc_med(C, beta, TAU)\\n            ms, ok = fm(th, mc, se)\\n            if not ok:\\n                all_ok = False\\n            print(f\\\"  {cn:<14} {fv(th)}  {fv(mc)}  {ms}\\\")\\n\\n        status = 'ALL MATCH' if all_ok else 'MISMATCH DETECTED'\\n        print(f\\\"  >>> {status}\\\")\\n\\n        t1 = time.time()\\n        v, s, a = rsearch_med(beta, TAU)\\n        t2 = time.time()\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n  Random search ({N_RANDOM//1000}k, {t2-t1:.1f}s): \\\"\\n              f\\\"violation={v:.1%}  sign_flip={s:.1%}  amplification={a:.1%}\\\")\\n\\n        # 2, 1] = .5 * q1[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 1] = .5 * q1[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 2, 2] = qm[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 3, 3] = qm[:, 2]\\n\\n    EZX = np.zeros((ns, 4, 4))\\n    EZX[:, 0, 0] = 1;       EZX[:, 0, 1] = .5\\n    EZX[:, 0, 2] = pm[1];   EZX[:, 0, 3] = pm[2]\\n    EZX[:, 1, 0] = .5;      EZX[:, 1, 1] = .5\\n    EZX[:, 1, 2] = .5 * p1[1]; EZX[:, 1, 3] = .5 * p1[2]\\n    EZX[:, 2, 0] = qm[:, 1];   EZX[:, 2, 1] = .5 * q1[:, 1]\\n    EZX[:, 2, 2] = Cs[:, 1, 1] * pm[1]; EZX[:, 2, 3] = Cs[:, 1, 2] * pm[2]\\n    EZX[:, 3, 0] = qm[:, 2];   EZX[:, 3, 1] = .5 * q1[:, 2]\\n    EZX[:, 3, 2] = Cs[:, 2, 1] * pm[1]; EZX[:, 3, 3] = Cs[:, 2, 2] * pm[2]\\n\\n    rhs = np.einsum('nij,j->ni', EZX, b)\\n    pl = np.linalg.solve(EZZ, rhs[:, :, None])[:, :, 0]\\n\\n    df = np.abs(pl - clean)\\n    viol = np.any(df > .01, 1).mean()\\n    nz = np.abs(clean) > 1e-6\\n    sign = np.any(np.sign(pl[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), 1).mean()\\n    amp = np.any(np.abs(pl) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, 1).mean()\\n    return viol, sign, amp\\n\\n\\n# ================================================================\\n# DAG 2  Front-door: T -> M -> Y,  U -> T,  U -> Y\\n#\\n# DGP:  U ~ N(0,1)\\n#        T = alpha_U * U + eta,  eta ~ N(0,1)\\n#        M | T ~ Cat(softmax(gamma + delta*T))\\n#        Y = beta1*D_{M,1} + beta2*D_{M,2} + lambda*U + eps\\n#        M* | M ~ Cat(C[:, M])\\n#\\n# Researcher regression:  Y ~ 1 + T + D*_{M,1} + D*_{M,2}\\n#\\n# T correlated with U (confounding), so E[Z'(lambda*U)] != 0.\\n# Use large-sample moment approximation:\\n#   plim = (E[Z'Z])^{-1} E[Z'Y]   estimated from N_LARGE samples.\\n#\\n# For fast random search, pre-compute sufficient statistics:\\n#   E[D_{M,k}], E[D_{M,k}*T], E[D_{M,k}*Y]  for k=0,1,2\\n# Then for any C:\\n#   E[D*_j] = sum_k C[j,k]*E[D_{M,k}]   etc.\\n# ================================================================\\ndef _gen_fd(N, beta, rng, g=GAMMA_FD, d=DELTA_FD,\\n            aU=ALPHA_U, lU=LAMBDA_U):\\n    U = rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    T = aU * U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    lo = np.column_stack([np.zeros(N), g[1] + d[1]*T, g[2] + d[2]*T])\\n    lo -= lo.max(1, keepdims=True)\\n    e = np.exp(lo)\\n    p = e / e.sum(1, keepdims=True)\\n    cu = np.cumsum(p, axis=1)\\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N)\\n    M = (u >= cu[:, 0]).astype(int) + (u >= cu[:, 1]).astype(int)\\n    DM1 = (M == 1).astype(float)\\n    DM2 = (M == 2).astype(float)\\n    Y = beta[1]*DM1 + beta[2]*DM2 + lU*U + rng.normal(0, 1, N)\\n    return T, M, Y, U\\n\\n\\ndef precomp_fd(beta, N=N_LARGE, seed=123):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    T, M, Y, _ = _gen_fd(N, beta, rng)\\n    DM = np.zeros((N, K))\\n    for k in range(K):\\n        DM[:, k] = (M == k).astype(float)\\n    return dict(\\n        E_T=T.mean(), E_T2=(T**2).mean(),\\n        E_Y=Y.mean(), E_TY=(T*Y).mean(),\\n        E_DM=DM.mean(0),\\n        E_DM_T=(DM * T[:, None]).mean(0),\\n        E_DM_Y=(DM * Y[:, None]).mean(0))\\n\\n\\ndef plim_fd(C, st):\\n    ds  = C @ st['E_DM']\\n    dst = C @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    dsy = C @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.array([\\n        [1,      ET,      ds[1],    ds[2]   ],\\n        [ET,     ET2,     dst[1],   dst[2]  ],\\n        [ds[1],  dst[1],  ds[1],    0       ],\\n        [ds[2],  dst[2],  0,        ds[2]   ]])\\n\\n    EZY = np.array([EY, ETY, dsy[1], dsy[2]])\\n    return np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY)\\n\\n\\ndef mc_fd(C, beta, nr=N_REPS, N=N_MC, s\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 428 字符] ...\\n\\n=N_RANDOM, seed=999):\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    clean = plim_fd(np.eye(K), st)\\n\\n    Cs = np.zeros((ns, K, K))\\n    for i in range(K):\\n        Cs[:, :, i] = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=ns)\\n\\n    E_DMs   = Cs @ st['E_DM']\\n    E_DMs_T = Cs @ st['E_DM_T']\\n    E_DMs_Y = Cs @ st['E_DM_Y']\\n    ET, ET2 = st['E_T'], st['E_T2']\\n    EY, ETY = st['E_Y'], st['E_TY']\\n\\n    EZZ = np.zeros((ns, 4, 4))\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 0] = 1;       EZZ[:, 0, 1] = EZZ[:, 1, 0] = ET\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 0] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 0, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 0] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 1] = ET2\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 2] = EZZ[:, 2, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 1, 3] = EZZ[:, 3, 1] = E_DMs_T[:, 2]\\n    EZZ[:, 2, 2] = E_DMs[:, 1]\\n    EZZ[:, 3, 3] = E_DMs[:, 2]\\n\\n    EZY = np.zeros((ns, 4))\\n    EZY[:, 0] = EY;   EZY[:, 1] = ETY\\n    EZY[:, 2] = E_DMs_Y[:, 1]\\n    EZY[:, 3] = E_DMs_Y[:, 2]\\n\\n    pl = np.linalg.solve(EZZ, EZY[:, :, None])[:, :, 0]\\n\\n    df = np.abs(pl - clean)\\n    viol = np.any(df > .01, 1).mean()\\n    nz = np.abs(clean) > 1e-6\\n    sign = np.any(np.sign(pl[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), 1).mean()\\n    amp = np.any(np.abs(pl) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, 1).mean()\\n    return viol, sign, amp\\n\\n\\n# ================================================================\\n# Main\\n# ================================================================\\ndef main():\\n    t0 = time.time()\\n    np.set_printoptions(precision=4, suppress=True)\\n\\n    for bn, beta in [(\\\"mixed [0,1,-0.5]\\\", BETA_MIXED),\\n                      (\\\"same [0,1,0.5]\\\",   BETA_SAME)]:\\n\\n        # ---- DAG 1: Mediation ----\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*90}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  MEDIATION: T -> A -> Y  |  beta={bn}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'='*90}\\\")\\n        p0, p1 = p_cat(0, GAMMA_MED, DELTA_MED), p_cat(1, GAMMA_MED, DELTA_MED)\\n        print(f\\\"  P(A|T=0)={fv(p0,6,3)}  P(A|T=1)={fv(p1,6,3)}  tau={TAU}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Coefs: [{', '.join(COEF_NAMES)}]\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n        hdr = f\\\"  {'C':<14} {'Theory':>36}  {'MC mean':>36}  Mt\\\"\\n        print(hdr)\\n        print(f\\\"  {'-'*92}\\\")\\n\\n        all_ok = True\\n        for cn, C in C_DICT.items():\\n            th = plim_med(C, beta, TAU)\\n            mc, se = mc_med(C, beta, TAU)\\n            ms, ok = fm(th, mc, se)\\n            if not ok:\\n                all_ok = False\\n            print(f\\\"  {cn:<14} {fv(th)}  {fv(mc)}  {ms}\\\")\\n\\n        status = 'ALL MATCH' if all_ok else 'MISMATCH DETECTED'\\n        print(f\\\"  >>> {status}\\\")\\n\\n        t1 = time.time()\\n        v, s, a = rsearch_med(beta, TAU)\\n        t2 = time.time()\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n  Random search ({N_RANDOM//1000}k, {t2-t1:.1f}s): \\\"\\n              f\\\"violation={v:.1%}  sign_flip={s:.1%}  amplification={a:.1%}\\\")\\n\\n        # ============================================================\nL[a:b] = \"\"\"\\\ndef rand_search_collider(n_search=100_000, N_ap=50_000, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n    T, Y, A = _gen_collider(N_ap, COLLIDER_PARAMS, rng)\n    u = rng.uniform(size=N_ap)\n    N = float(N_ap)\n    sT, sTT, sY, sTY = T.sum(), (T*T).sum(), Y.sum(), (T*Y).sum()\n\n    clean = _fast_ols_all(T, Y, A, N, sT, sTT, sY, sTY)\n    ones = np.ones(N_ap)\n    tau_nc = _ols(np.column_stack([ones, T]), Y)[1]\n\n    rng_s = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1)\n    coefs = np.empty((n_search, 4))\n    for i in range(n_search):\n        C = rng_s.dirichlet([1, 1, 1], size=3).T\n        Astar = _generate_Astar_fixed_u(A, C, u)\n        coefs[i] = _fast_ols_all(T, Y, Astar, N, sT, sTT, sY, sTY)\n\n    df = np.abs(coefs - clean)\n    viol = np.any(df > .01, axis=1).mean()\n    nz = np.abs(clean) > 1e-6\n    sign = np.any(np.sign(coefs[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), axis=1).mean()\n    amp = np.any(np.abs(coefs) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, axis=1).mean()\n\n    tau = COLLIDER_PARAMS['tau']\n    biases = coefs[:, 1] - tau\n    true_bias = clean[1] - tau\n    nc_bias = tau_nc - tau\n    ctrl_worse = (np.abs(biases) > np.abs(nc_bias)).mean()\n    misclass_amp = (np.abs(biases) > np.abs(true_bias)).mean()\n\n    return viol, sign, amp, biases, nc_bias, true_bias, ctrl_worse, misclass_amp\n\n\n\"\"\".splitlines(keepends=True)\n\n# ============================================================\n# Step 5: Insert _fast_ols_all before rand_search_collider\n# ============================================================\nidx = fl('def rand_search_collider')\nL[idx:idx] = \"\"\"\\\ndef _fast_ols_all(T, Y, A_star, N, sT, sTT, sY, sTY):\n    m1 = A_star == 1\n    m2 = A_star == 2\n    n1 = float(m1.sum())\n    n2 = float(m2.sum())\n    tD1 = T[m1].sum()\n    tD2 = T[m2].sum()\n    yD1 = Y[m1].sum()\n    yD2 = Y[m2].sum()\n    ZZ = np.array([[N, sT, n1, n2],\n                   [sT, sTT, tD1, tD2],\n                   [n1, tD1, n1, 0.0],\n                   [n2, tD2, 0.0, n2]])\n    ZY = np.array([sY, sTY, yD1, yD2])\n    return np.linalg.solve(ZZ, ZY)\n\n\n\"\"\".splitlines(keepends=True)\n\nwith open(P, 'w') as f:\n    f.writelines(L)\n\nprint(f\"OK: all 5 patches applied. {orig_len} -> {len(L)} lines\")\n",
    "structuredPatch": [],
    "originalFile": null,
    "userModified": false
  }
}

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested backporting the violation metric methodology from `plan_001_mediation_frontdoor.py` to `plan_001_collider_mbias_iv.py`. The mediation/front-door script's metric design (C=I baseline comparison → violation/sign_flip/amplification rates) was reviewer-approved, and needs to be unified across both scripts. The task specified 5 steps: (1) read mediation script, (2) read collider script, (3) align metric definitions, (4) rerun and verify, (5) update registry. IV metrics were explicitly excluded from changes due to structural differences (Wald/OLS).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **C=I baseline comparison**: Using identity confusion matrix (no misclassification) as reference point for all violation metrics
   - **Violation metric**: `np.any(np.abs(coefs - clean) > .01, axis=1).mean()` — any of 4 OLS coefficients deviating >0.01 from C=I baseline
   - **Sign flip metric**: `np.any(np.sign(coefs[:, nz]) != np.sign(clean[nz]), axis=1).mean()` — sign reversal vs C=I for non-zero coefficients
   - **Amplification metric**: `np.any(np.abs(coefs) > np.abs(clean) + 1e-6, axis=1).mean()` — magnitude exceeds C=I baseline
   - **_fast_ols_all vs _fast_ols_tau**: Original `_fast_ols_tau` only returned tau coefficient (index 1); new `_fast_ols_all` returns all 4 OLS coefficients for the regression Y ~ 1 + T + D1 + D2
   - **Collider/M-bias DAG specifics**: In these DAGs, controlling for the collider/M-bias variable is the researcher error. The C=I baseline already has bias from this incorrect control. Misclassification changes the magnitude of this pre-existing bias.
   - **Supplementary metrics preserved**: `ctrl_worse` (|bias_ctrl(A*)| > |bias_nc|) and `misclass_amplifies` (|bias_ctrl(A*)| > |bias_ctrl(trueA)|) kept as collider/M-bias-specific additions
   - **Random search approach**: 100k random confusion matrices C (each column drawn from Dirichlet), fixed base dataset for efficiency

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/plan_001_mediation_frontdoor.py`** (read-only reference)
     - Source of the "good" violation metric design
     - Key functions: `rsearch_med()` and `rsearch_fd()` which compute violation/sign_flip/amplification against C=I baseline
     - Output format: `violation={v:.1%}  sign_flip={s:.1%}  amplification={a:.1%}`

   - **`/home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/plan_001_collider_mbias_iv.py`** (modified, 518→557 lines)
     - Target of the backport
     - **New function added** `_fast_ols_all` at line 248:
       ```python
       def _fast_ols_all(T, Y, A_star, N, sT, sTT, sY, sTY):
           m1 = A_star == 1
           m2 = A_star == 2
           n1 = float(m1.sum())
           n2 = float(m2.sum())
           tD1 = T[m1].sum()
           tD2 = T[m2].sum()
           yD1 = Y[m1].sum()
           yD2 = Y[m2].sum()
           ZZ = np.array([[N, sT, n1, n2],
                          [sT, sTT, tD1, tD2],
                          [n1, tD1, n1, 0.0],
                          [n2, tD2, 0.0, n2]])
           ZY = np.array([sY, sTY, yD1, yD2])
           return np.linalg.solve(ZZ, ZY)
       ```
     - **Modified `rand_search_collider`** (line 265): Now computes all 4 OLS coefficients per random C, calculates violation/sign_flip/amplification vs `clean = _fast_ols_all(T, Y, A, N, ...)` (C=I baseline). Returns `(viol, sign, amp, biases, nc_bias, true_bias, ctrl_worse, misclass_amp)`.
     - **Modified `rand_search_mbias`** (line 299): Same pattern as collider but using `_gen_mbias` and `MBIAS_PARAMS`.
     - **Modified main() collider output** (line ~415-420): Now prints unified format:

       Supplementary: ctrl_worse={cw:.1%}  misclass_amplifies={ma:.1%}
